Runtime-only commit, no [require-build]: configs/firmus-framework.json now
pins images.sglang to the image built by A1 (Actions 39070273796, commit
0374c55, /scratch/home/ACCOUNT/qwen/containers/39070273796.sqsh, sha256
7c6286e5..., 12249706496 bytes). scripts/select-qwen-container.sh finds
that archive along first parents, and provision-firmus-framework.sh
requires a reused image to equal the config pin, so the pin has to move
with the reuse. Nothing else changes: same patches, same placement file,
same reference config.

Why a second run of the same image: same-config Firmus runs differ by
about 3% (6521.98 vs 6699.30), so the bundle is judged on two runs of its
image interleaved with a reference rerun. So far: R2 (reference config on
image e29dfde, 39069954937) scored 6485.02 and A1 (this image, first run)
7251.58, with ITL > 20 ms failures 106 -> 7, TTFT > 3 s failures 114 -> 104,
Prefill MegaMoE (main geometry) 2.152 -> 1.820 ms and Decode MegaMoE
0.2491 -> 0.2126 ms in the profile pass. A2 checks that this is repeatable
and not run-to-run noise.
